Define the goals and constraints that remain binding, who can depart from the ordinary route, and how the decision and its consequences are communicated and reviewed.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>Case facts invalidate the normal route or expertise is needed beyond a fixed activity sequence.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>For low-risk, simple work, a clear goal and accessible expert may suffice without a case-management platform.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>Competent actors, intelligible constraints and a way to communicate and review consequential departures.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/ea.md#r-ea616c2415553e86b08efdff373144725122a88625921c0b569a2bcbb3f69ca4">Complete shared assumptions · ea616c241555</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>A case worker’s discretion is bounded by professional and organisational authority; a model’s optional task does not confer legal or ethical permission.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Communicate exceptional facts, chosen departure and unresolved obligations to those affected or responsible.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/b8.md#r-b87b187097b1b4be6cb4766e6efed91fb2651ed2d0e90acb72dc4562802636c1">Complete shared consumer · b87b187097b1</a></p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>The case can proceed or stop for defensible reasons without losing necessary accountability.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>Rigid rules cause harm; unrestricted discretion causes inconsistency, bias or untraceable decisions.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>Rigid rules cause harm; unrestricted discretion causes inconsistency, bias or untraceable decisions.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p>S026 criticises bureaucratic formalism; S030 raises fairness and accountability. ## EBPM:EBPM-039

<dl><dt>Key</dt><dd><p><a href="properties-004.md#EBPM-EBPM-039">EBPM:EBPM-039</a></p></dd><dt>Lineage</dt><dd><p>EBPM</p></dd><dt>Local id</dt><dd><p>EBPM-039</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Incentive-aware appraisal and metric interpretation</p></dd><dt>Origin class</dt><dd><dl><dt>Type</dt><dd><p>RESEARCH_EXTRACTION_AND_CRITICISM_ADJUDICATION</p></dd><dt>Discovery family</dt><dd><p>B7</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>S005</p></li><li><p>S024</p></li><li><p>S030</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Note</dt><dd><p>An examined candidate is not a pre-approved obligation. Stress propositions are not attributed to the whole field; composition properties are this researcher’s synthesis.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_WITH_ANALYTICAL_REFINEMENT</p></dd><dt>Disposition</dt><dd><p>USEFUL_BUT_EASILY_GAMED</p></dd><dt>Examination status</dt><dd><p>EXAMINED_WITH_EVIDENCE_LIMIT</p></dd><dt>Evidence limit</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/bc.md#r-bc4c62b5c804840d72fb87068bc83194c82005c3bea0984221ecd1b22e62922f">Complete shared evidence limit · bc4c62b5c804</a></p></dd><dt>Bearer</dt><dd><p>Process visibility, appraisal, incentives and affected-person protection</p></dd><dt>Bearer annotation provenance</dt><dd><p>BWP analytical subject annotation; read with unaltered original DOMAIN_PROFILE, not a revision of the constituent criterion.</p></dd><dt>Criterion</dt><dd><p>An appraisal or improvement decision accounts for known distortions and does not equate target attainment with the entire outcome.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Interpret indicators in light of the behaviour they reward, omitted work and differences in case difficulty. Use multiple relevant observations and inquiry into gaming rather than assuming the measured quantity remains a neutral description once tied to appraisal.</p></dd><dt>Trigger</dt><dd><p>A measure affects pay, ranking, sanctions, targets or resource access.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger cheap path</dt><dd><p>Use qualitative review or aggregate learning measures where precise individual attribution is not warranted.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites required input</dt><dd><p>Knowledge of incentives, case mix and how work can move outside the measurement boundary.</p></dd><dt>Assumptions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/08.md#r-0860500769c331177433b39664f2ec51b4885712e75e9eaa7c097a75dc5c905f">Complete shared assumptions · 0860500769c3</a></p></dd><dt>Authority</dt><dd><p>Appraisal decision-makers are accountable for the use of measures; analysts must expose limitations rather than authorising sanctions.</p></dd><dt>Communication interaction</dt><dd><p>Workers and recipients can explain discrepant indicators and hidden burden without the discrepancy alone proving misconduct.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/32.md#r-328cf3ed0f44e643b31ae33a61147d94fae0769238aaa6dfbd8763886a75fab4">Complete shared consumer · 328cf3ed0f44</a></p></dd><dt>Output postcondition</dt><dd><p>An appraisal or improvement decision accounts for known distortions and does not equate target attainment with the entire outcome.</p></dd><dt>Failure addressed</dt><dd><p>Gaming, cherry-picking easy cases, premature closure, hidden rework and suppression of bad news.</p></dd><dt>Costs failure modes</dt><dd><p>Gaming, cherry-picking easy cases, premature closure, hidden rework and suppression of bad news.</p></dd><dt>Criticism</dt><dd><p>The direct causal magnitude of incentive effects is not estimated by the inspected BPM evidence; this is a source-grounded analytical safeguard, not a validated universal scoring scheme.</p></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-001.md#EBPM-S005">EBPM:S005</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-003.md#EBPM-S024">EBPM:S024</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-003.md#EBPM-S026">EBPM:S026</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-003.md#EBPM-S030">EBPM:S030</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-005.md#EBPM-S041">EBPM:S041</a></p></li><li><p><a href="../source-register/sources-005.md#EBPM-S049">EBPM:S049</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>Within lineage relation ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EBPM:R026</p></li><li><p>EBPM:R027</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Retirement omission condition</dt><dd><dl><dt>Current native cheap or nontrigger</dt><dd><p>Use qualitative review or aggregate learning measures where precise individual attribution is not warranted.</p></dd><dt>Rule</dt><dd><p>Do not add or retain a mechanism whose actual trigger, consumer or protected dependency is absent. Verify that actors can perform the intended work and that the organisation receives the outcome; deployment is only one observation.</p></dd><dt>Ceremony vs property</dt><dd><dl><dt>Protected function</dt><dd><p>Real cases are completed under the new arrangement with responsibilities and observed consequences understood.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p>Sponsors resource change; owners coordinate adoption; technical teams control only technical deployment within their mandate.</p></dd><dt>Minimal adequate alternative</dt><dd><p>An organisational agreement, training or information change may suffice without new software.</p></dd><dt>Fuller form trigger</dt><dd><p>A to-be design is introduced or a new system is claimed to improve a process.</p></dd><dt>Prerequisites</dt><dd><p>Operational readiness, integration with surrounding work, accountable authority and an evaluation baseline.</p></dd><dt>Simplification risk</dt><dd><p>Technically live but unused system; abandoned implementation; shadow work; disruption of necessary informal coordination.</p></dd><dt>Omission or retirement condition</dt><dd><p>Omit when the stated trigger is absent, an existing/cheaper mechanism supplies the required function, or its risk/consumer/dependency has disappeared. removing it must not destroy a real protective or accountability function.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Current status</dt><dd><p>RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM</p></dd><dt>Property kind</dt><dd><p>CRITERION_COUPLED_TO_OPERATING_METHOD</p></dd><dt>Evidence strength partitions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/c7.md#r-c79d5d5b599ba2c477604f85a6e19de87f02ef07be6bf53284c6fb2791ebadb2">Complete shared EVIDENCE STRENGTH PARTITIONS · c79d5d5b599b</a></p></dd><dt>Primary source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>S002</p></li><li><p>S005</p></li><li><p>S027</p></li><li><p>S043</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Critical source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>S027</p></li><li><p>S041</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Empirical or case source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>S002</p></li><li><p>S005</p></li><li><p>S027</p></li><li><p>S041</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Contrary evidence</dt><dd><dl><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>S027</p></li><li><p>S041</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Adjudication</dt><dd><p>S027 followed 25 intended processes but only seven implemented processes were evaluated;
